Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The isotopes operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 536. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 973 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^973(S, F) is trivial. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the isotopes structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 317.
